## §0 Test account prerequisites

| Requirement | Detail |
|-------------|--------|
| Brand | `symply-budget` staging/Debug (`com.symply.budget`, scheme `simplebudget://`) |
| API | Budget Worker staging (`simple-budget-api-staging.*`) — dev app → staging |
| Credentials | `e2e/credentials.local` — account with completed year setup + categories |
| Household | Active HH with budget year, optional savings goals, optional utility accounts |
| Soft Transfer | House consent for read-only ST UI; **throwaway HH** for import/export mutations |
| Launch | `e2e/maestro/subflows/budget-launch-logged-in.yaml` |
| Login | `e2e/maestro/subflows/budget-login-email-if-needed.yaml` |
| Chat | Budget chat rooms seeded or empty-state acceptable |
| Savings kill-switch | Default enabled; set `CONFIG_KV savings_enabled=false` only for CORNER rows |
| Device | iOS Simulator; Metro `:8081` with `APP_BRAND=symply-budget` |
| Console verify | `__DEV__` Metro network log — method, path, status (no secrets/PII in notes) |

---

## Automated Test Coverage — Execution Groups

Groups are independently runnable unless a dependency is noted. **Run order:** G0 → G1 → G2–G11 (any order) → G13/G14 (no simulator) → **G12 last** (destructive Soft Transfer). Every group that mutates restores its fixtures before exiting; G12 additionally requires a throwaway household so a failed restore cannot corrupt the shared fixture.

**Account / device topology.** A single logged-in account on one iOS Simulator covers G0–G10 and G13. Two accounts in the same household are required wherever collaboration or role behaviour is asserted (G8 wish attribution, G9 chat participants and mentions, G11 owner-vs-member actions). Two brands on two simulators are required only for the House↔Budget round trip, which is **not** covered here — see the Flagged section.

| # | Group | Rows (ID list) | Device / accounts | Fixtures & teardown | Notes |
|---|-------|----------------|-------------------|---------------------|-------|
| 0 | Auth boundary | `BUDGET-AUTH-001` … `014` | Fresh sim or logged-out | None; leaves the session logged in for later groups | **Run first.** `AUTH-004` (logout) must run last within the group. `AUTH-009` stops at the Apple sheet (2FA). `AUTH-014` is a security negative — needs a second household id. |
| 1 | Smoke | `BUDGET-SMOKE-*` | Logged-in fixture HH | Read-only | **Never** taps `run-transfer-button`. Skips gracefully when unauthenticated. |
| 2 | Dashboard load & controls | `BUDGET-DASH-001` … `044` | Logged-in; staging API | Read-only except `DASH-028/029/030` — delete the duplicated item afterwards | Includes the AI gate, the savings-404 probe, and the `formatBudgetCurrency` rows (`040`–`043`) which are pure-formatter and need no network. |
| 3 | Planning, item form & transfer | `BUDGET-PLAN-001` … `108` | Logged-in | Creates planned items and one transfer; delete the items and undo the transfer on exit | Largest group. `PLAN-059`–`063` are the money-validation core. `PLAN-072/073` are API-only and can run without a simulator. `PLAN-074`–`096` are the sub-budgets feature (`PLAN-087` = the monthly-overview 500 regression). `PLAN-097`–`106` are the "All planned" explorer (`budget-see-all-planned.yaml`, read-only); `PLAN-107/108` are Planned↔Spending parity (reminder; quick-add now = parity by absence, see SPEND-076). |
| 4 | Spending & receipts | `BUDGET-SPEND-001` … `103` | Logged-in | Creates expenses; delete them on exit | `SPEND-041/042` are API-only. Receipt camera is stubbed on CI — use the gallery subflow. `SPEND-076` guards the REMOVED list quick-add row (`077` is the form-side prefill; `078/079` are retired with it). `090`–`096` are the sales-tax attribution + multi-file receipt work; `097`–`103` are the select-mode/batch-copy/batch-delete rows (renumbered 2026-07-31 — they collided with `090`–`096` under the same IDs since the day both features shipped). |
| 5 | Savings & registered | `BUDGET-SAVE-001` … `077` | Logged-in; savings enabled | Creates income/spending/goal/recurring rows; delete on exit | **Skip the whole group** if the savings kill-switch returns 404. `SAVE-076` reconciles against the Budget overview and must run after G2. |
| 6 | Pension | `BUDGET-PEN-001` … `049` | Logged-in; savings enabled | Import commit → **undo** in the same run (`PEN-045/046`) | `PEN-048` is destructive bulk-delete — use a throwaway member. `PEN-049` is a route-shadowing guard, API-only. |
| 7 | Bills & utilities | `BUDGET-BILL-001` … `042` | Logged-in | Creates bills/accounts; delete on exit | 409 duplicate rows need a bill already present for the same account+period. `BILL-037`–`041` are API-only. |
| 8 | Wishes | `BUDGET-WISH-001` … `032` | Logged-in **+ second member** for `WISH-014` | Creates a wish and entries; delete the wish on exit | Image upload uses the real fixture manifest. `WISH-026`–`030` are API-only cap checks. |
| 9 | Chat & AI | `BUDGET-BCHAT-001` … `046` | Logged-in **+ second member** for participants/mentions | Creates a room; delete it on exit (`BCHAT-044`) | Needs one AI-enabled and one AI-disabled room to separate `BCHAT-036` from `037`. `BCHAT-042` must not log the WS token. |
| 10 | Settings & More | `BUDGET-SETT-001` … `032` | Logged-in | Saves a month goal; restore the prior value on exit | `SETT-026` asserts a **silent** failure path — expect no user feedback. |
| 10b | Local-first V2 | `BUDGET-LF-001` … `016` | Logged-in; `EXPO_PUBLIC_BUDGET_LOCAL_FIRST` / Budget `__DEV__` | Creates local expenses / backup share; `LF-011` replaces the local household with the restore-smoke fixture; `LF-012` writes an archive into app Documents; `LF-013` enables then disables a backup schedule | Sync chrome + invite + offline CRUD + no financial D1 + backup (own `BudgetBackupScreen`) + destinations + scheduled auto-backup + restore UI + Soft Transfer + AI ladders. |
| 10c | Local-first multi-member | `BUDGET-MM-001` … `011` | **Two simulators, two accounts** (`E2E_EMAIL` + `E2E_EMAIL_SECONDARY`) | Creates a spending on each device; `MM-011` deletes both on both devices | Own runner — `scripts/e2e/run-budget-multi-member-sync.sh`, NOT part of the single-device budget suite. Enrolment is a chain: MM-004 cannot approve a claim MM-003 never made. |
| 11 | Households | `BUDGET-HH-001` … `029` | Admin-capable account **+ a member account** for `HH-024` | Creates a throwaway household; delete it on exit | `HH-022/023/028` are destructive — never run against the shared fixture household. |
| 12 | Soft Transfer (destructive, last) | `BUDGET-ST-001` … `025` | Throwaway HH + House consent | Re-grant any consent revoked by `ST-019`; restore the throwaway HH | **Run after G0–G11.** The smoke suite must skip this group entirely. |
| 13 | Corner contracts | `BUDGET-CORNER-001` … `020` | Mixed fixtures / API tests | Read-only except `CORNER-016/017` | `CORNER-011` is the money-rule sweep and touches seven screens — budget time for it. `CORNER-017` needs airplane mode toggling. |
| 14 | Unit / API only | Every row whose Layer is `Unit` or `API` with no Maestro path | Jest / Vitest / Worker | None | No simulator required; safe to run in CI on every commit. |

The browser opens with its root crumb and then branches: **signed out → a reconnect button** (never a bare retry, which would just reproduce the failure), **signed in → `New folder` + `Save backups here`**. Cancelling changes nothing | `budget-backup-drive-folder-row`, `budget-backup-drive-folder-value`, `budget-backup-drive-account-row`, `budget-folder-picker`, `budget-folder-crumb-0`, `budget-folder-picker-{error,retry,loading,cancel}`, `budget-folder-{new,use-here}`, `budget-backup-drive-{switch-account,disconnect}` | AsyncStorage `budget.backup.driveFolder` (id + trail + `source`); Drive `files.list`/`files.create` under the `drive` scope | Google Drive v3 (assertion-only — Maestro cannot drive Google's consent screen) | Maestro, Unit | `e2e/maestro/budget/budget-local-first-backup-drive-folder.yaml`, `src/features/budget/local/__tests__/backupDestinations.test.ts` | **VISIBLE** — read-only; never completes OAuth and never picks a folder, so it cannot move where a real member's backups go. Both signed-in and signed-out states pass, via `runFlow.when`. |

## MM — Local-first multi-member (two simulators, two accounts)

Driven by [`scripts/e2e/run-budget-multi-member-sync.sh`](../../../scripts/e2e/run-budget-multi-member-sync.sh).
Device **A** = owner (`Budget-A`, `E2E_EMAIL`), device **B** = invitee
(`Budget-B`, `E2E_EMAIL_SECONDARY`). Only one device is under Maestro at a
time. The runner scrapes the invite code off the Metro log and types it into B —
that is the harness standing in for the person who passes the code along; every
other step is real UI. See [BUDGET_MULTI_MEMBER_SYNC_E2E.md](../BUDGET_MULTI_MEMBER_SYNC_E2E.md).

Login with that device's account | Dashboard for the named account | `login-screen`, `budget-screen` | House auth proxy | n/a | Maestro | `e2e/maestro/budget-multi-member/mm-01-signin.yaml` | Runs on BOTH devices with different credentials |
| BUDGET-MM-002 | Owner creates a household invite | 1. Device Sync → Sync now 2. Invite & Household → Create an invite | "Invite created" with short code | `budget-sync-screen`, `budget-invite-screen`, `budget-settings-create-invite` | POST `/v2/households/:id/invites` 201 | `[E2E-INVITE]` line (dev-only) | Maestro | `mm-02-owner-create-invite.yaml` | **MUTATION** — control plane only |

Confirm replacement | "Request sent"; join button reads "Waiting for approval…" | `budget-invite-screen`, `budget-settings-join-link/-code/-secret/-join-household` | GET `/v2/invites/lookup`, POST …/claim | n/a | Maestro | `mm-03-member-join.yaml` | **MUTATION** — replaces B's local ledger by design |
| BUDGET-MM-004 | Owner approves via OOB word | 1. Invite & Household → See who is waiting 2. Tap the word the invitee read out 3. Device Sync → Sync now (key deposit) | "Device approved"; HDK deposited | `budget-invite-screen`, `budget-settings-join-requests`, `budget-settings-approve-phrase-N`, `budget-sync-screen` | POST …/approve, mailbox deposit | n/a | Maestro | `mm-04-owner-approve.yaml` | Wrong word must fail — real OOB check |
| BUDGET-MM-005 | Invitee receives the household key | 1. Device Sync → Sync now, until Invite & Household stops saying "Waiting for approval…" | "Waiting for approval…" clears; writes unpause | `budget-sync-screen`, `budget-settings-sync-now`, `budget-invite-screen`, `budget-settings-join-household` | mailbox fetch + `installHouseholdKeys` | n/a | Maestro | `mm-05-member-enrol-sync.yaml` | Retries — deposit and sync are not synchronised |

### MM-020…023 — both devices driven CONCURRENTLY

Phases 2–5 launch a Maestro process per device at the same time. No rendezvous
channel is needed because each flow rendezvouses on the data: it does its own
half, then polls sync until the PEER's change appears, so whichever device
arrives first waits inside its own poll loop. Only sign-in (`MM-001`) is
serial — it is each device's first invocation, so it is where the XCUITest
driver cold-starts, and starting both at once makes the two drivers kill each
other. The second device's launch is staggered by `MM_STAGGER_SECONDS` (25s)
for the same reason.

| ID | Description | Steps | Expected | UI elements | BE / persistence | Console verify | Layer | Automation | Notes |
|----|----|----|----|----|----|----|----|----|----|
| BUDGET-MM-020 | Concurrent creates in two different sections | Both: add a row (A in Spending, B in Planning) 2. Both: sync until the peer's row shows | Each device ends holding BOTH members' rows | `budget-add-spent`, `budget-add-planned` | local op log + mailbox | n/a | Maestro | `mm-20-add-and-await-peer.yaml` | **MUTATION** — different entity types on purpose: proves projection is not spending-only |
| BUDGET-MM-021 | Concurrent modifies propagate | Both: edit their own row's amount 2. Both: sync until the peer's NEW value shows | Peer's updated amount visible on the other device | `budget-edit-spent`, `budget-edit-planned` | field-level delta | n/a | Maestro | `mm-21-modify-and-await-peer.yaml` | **MUTATION** — an edit ships changed fields onto a row the peer already holds; a create does not test this |
| BUDGET-MM-022 | Concurrent deletes propagate | Both: delete their own row 2. Both: sync until the peer's row disappears | Peer's row gone on the other device | `budget-delete-spent`, `budget-delete-planned` | tombstone sync | runner swipes the tray + taps the native confirm via `idb` (`lib/idb-row.sh`) | Maestro + runner | `mm-22a-delete-open-confirm.yaml`, `mm-22b-delete-verify-peer.yaml` | **MUTATION** — the op that must REMOVE state on the peer; a failed tombstone looks fine locally. Split around the gesture because Maestro can neither open the tray reliably nor see the `UIAlertController` confirm |
| BUDGET-MM-023 | Same row edited by both, offline, at once | Both: block network 2. Both: set their own amount 3. Both: unblock + sync | Both devices settle on the SAME amount; a merge-conflict indicator appears | `budget-item-cost-exact`, `budget-sync-conflicts` | LWW merge | runner reads both devices via `maestro hierarchy` | Maestro + runner | `mm-23-conflict-parallel.yaml` | **MUTATION** — asserts NO fixed winner: with simultaneous edits the winner is whichever HLC is higher, so pinning one would assert machine timing, not §8.4 |

## MORT — Mortgage rate history & impact

Per-statement interest-rate capture (`mortgage_rate_periods`, migration 0118) and the
History-tab change timeline + impact charts. See
[Mortgage_Implementation_Plan.md §5.1, §8.3](../../../requirements/as-built/mortgage/Mortgage_Implementation_Plan.md).

GET rate-periods. | BOTH sub-periods persist as rows, oldest first, with the lender's exact effective dates and the SIGNED variance (−86). A variable statement changes rate mid-period, so collapsing it to one headline rate loses the change date. | n/a | POST /households/:householdId/mortgage/:mortgageId/statements → `mortgage_rate_periods` | POST 201, GET 200 | API | `backend/src/routes/__tests__/mortgage.test.ts` | **MUTATION** — bps + signed variance |

GET rate-periods. | Exactly one period is derived, dated at the statement date — manual entries and pre-breakdown extractions must not silently vanish from the rate history. | manual statement form | POST …/statements | POST 201 | API | `backend/src/routes/__tests__/mortgage.test.ts` | **CORNER** — derived fallback |

GET rate-periods. | Exactly one row survives. > **2026-07-31 audit finding (see Flagged item 31).** Every Maestro/FE-Unit citation in this section previously pointed at files that do not exist anywhere in the repo: `e2e/maestro/budget/budget-bills.yaml`, `budget-bills-states.yaml` and `src/screens/budget/__tests__/BudgetBillsView.test.tsx` — there is no `BudgetBillsView` component. The real screens are the **shared** `src/screens/utilities/*` (`UtilitiesScreen`, `UtilityBillsScreen`, `PropertyTaxScreen`, …), reached through `BudgetNavigator`'s own stack routes — but `brands/symply-budget/brand.cjs`'s `tabs` pool contains no `utilities`/`bills` entry at all, and `app/(tabs)/bills.tsx` hard-redirects `/bills` to Home with a comment stating Bills was **removed from Budget** as a House-domain feature. No Budget-tagged Maestro flow exercises these screens, and none of the testIDs previously cited (`budget-bills-view`, `budget-bills-provider-*`, `budget-bills-action-*`) exist under those names anywhere in `src/` or `e2e/`. The rows below are corrected in place: FE/Maestro citations flip to `gap` with a note; the **BE/API rows are untouched** — `backend/src/routes/utilities.ts` and its tests are real and independent of whether the client can currently reach the screen.

## BCHAT — Budget chat, AI assistant, FAB

> **Shared chat module (2026-07-22).** Budget chat is no longer a fork. Both the frontend (`src/features/chat/` — screens, store, socket, api, navigator, FAB, all config-driven) and the backend (`backend/src/services/chat/chat-room-service-core.ts` + `backend/src/routes/chat/create-chat-routes.ts`) are now ONE implementation shared with House (and any future app). Budget = the shared code instantiated with `budgetChatConfig` (route segment `budget-chat-rooms`, `budget_chat_*` tables, `budget_chat_*` notifications, FAB presentation); House = `houseChatConfig` (tab presentation). **Data stays isolated per app** (separate tables + notification namespaces); only the code is shared, so every BCHAT behavior here is now identically available in House/Kaizen/Health chat, and a fix in one place applies to all. Colors follow each brand's theme automatically. Adding a new app's chat = one FE config in `src/features/chat/configs.ts` + one BE instantiation + a `<app>_chat_*` migration; nothing else. The unit tests referenced below live under `src/features/chat/**/__tests__/` and exercise the shared module via `budgetChatConfig`.

## NAV — Navigation, route guards & deep links

The Budget stack host (`src/navigation/BudgetNavigator.tsx`), the `app/budget-chat.tsx` route guard and the legacy `app/(tabs)/budget.tsx` deep-link shim carried **zero** test coverage before 2026-07-20. Notification and widget deep links land through this shim, so a regression here silently drops every inbound link.

Mount `BudgetNavigator` under the Budget brand. | All 30 screens named in `FULL_BUDGET_STACK_ROUTES` are registered on the stack. A screen listed in `mode.ts` but missing from the navigator is an unreachable route — assert the two lists agree in **both** directions. | stack | none | n/a (local) | Unit | `src/navigation/__tests__/BudgetNavigator.test.tsx` | **LOAD** — list-agreement guard |

## WIDGET — Home-screen widget (iOS)

`ios/SymplyEcosystemWidget/SymplyBudgetWidgetContent.swift` reads the App Group key `widget_budget_summary`. Before 2026-07-20 the **only** writer was `BudgetHomeScreen.tsx`, which `app/(tabs)/index.tsx` replaced with `BudgetNavigator` — so the key was never written and the widget always rendered its placeholder. Rows below assert the repaired publisher end to end.

| ID | Description | Steps | Expected | UI elements | BE / persistence | Console verify | Layer | Automation | Notes |
|----|----|----|----|----|----|----|----|----|----|

Open the Budget Home tab with a loaded monthly overview. | `widget_budget_summary` is written from the **mounted** dashboard, not from the dead `BudgetHomeScreen`. This is the regression that made the widget permanently blank. | Home tab | App Group `widget_budget_summary` | n/a (local) | Unit | `src/features/budget/__tests__/budgetSnapshot.test.ts`, `src/features/budget/__tests__/useBudgetSnapshotPublisher.test.tsx` | **MUTATION** — pairs with NAV-004 |
| BUDGET-WIDGET-002 | Snapshot payload matches the Swift contract | 1. Build a snapshot from a known overview. | Keys are exactly `remaining_cents`, `spent_cents`, `budget_cents`, `period_label` in snake_case — `BudgetSummary` decodes with `.convertFromSnakeCase`, so a camelCase key decodes to `nil` and blanks the widget. | n/a | App Group | n/a (local) | Unit | `src/features/budget/__tests__/budgetSnapshot.test.ts` | **CORNER** — cross-language contract |
| BUDGET-WIDGET-003 | Amounts are published as integer cents | 1. Publish an overview with `remainingBudget = 123456`. | The snapshot carries `123456`, not `1234.56` — `BudgetSummary.money(_ cents: Int, …)` divides by 100 itself, so publishing dollars under-reports by 100×. | n/a | App Group | n/a (local) | Unit | `src/features/budget/__tests__/budgetSnapshot.test.ts` | **CORNER** — unit mismatch |
| BUDGET-WIDGET-004 | No snapshot is written before the overview loads | 1. Mount the dashboard with `overview === null`. | Nothing is written — a zeroed snapshot would flash "$0 remaining" on the widget before real data arrives. | n/a | App Group | n/a (local) | Unit | `src/features/budget/__tests__/budgetSnapshot.test.ts`, `src/features/budget/__tests__/useBudgetSnapshotPublisher.test.tsx` | **VALIDATION** |

## WATCH — Apple Watch companion

`ios/SymplyEcosystemWatch/Views/SymplyBudgetWatchView.swift` reads the App Group key `watch_budget_today`. Before 2026-07-20 **nothing in the repo wrote any `watch_*` key** for any brand, so every watch view rendered its empty state permanently. Rows below assert the repaired publisher.

| ID | Description | Steps | Expected | UI elements | BE / persistence | Console verify | Layer | Automation | Notes |
|----|----|----|----|----|----|----|----|----|----|
| BUDGET-WATCH-001 | The dashboard publishes `watch_budget_today` | 1. Open the Budget Home tab with a loaded overview. | The key is written. Before the fix no writer existed and `SymplyBudgetWatchView` always showed "No budget yet. Open Symply Budget on your iPhone." | Home tab | App Group `watch_budget_today` | n/a (local) | Unit | `src/features/budget/__tests__/budgetSnapshot.test.ts`, `src/features/budget/__tests__/useBudgetSnapshotPublisher.test.tsx` | **MUTATION** — the core watch defect |
| BUDGET-WATCH-002 | Watch payload matches the Swift `BudgetToday` contract | 1. Build a watch snapshot from a known overview. | Field names and types match `BudgetToday`'s `CodingKeys` exactly, including `currencyCode` and the optional next-bill fields. A missing required key makes `load()` return `nil` and shows the empty state. | n/a | App Group | n/a (local) | Unit | `src/features/budget/__tests__/budgetSnapshot.test.ts` | **CORNER** — cross-language contract |
| BUDGET-WATCH-003 | Watch amounts are published as major units | 1. Publish an overview of 123456 cents. | `BudgetToday.budget` is `Double` and `currency(_ value:)` formats it **directly** — unlike the widget, the watch expects major units, so 123456 cents publishes as `1234.56`. Assert the two surfaces differ deliberately. | n/a | App Group | n/a (local) | Unit | `src/features/budget/__tests__/budgetSnapshot.test.ts` | **CORNER** — deliberate unit divergence from WIDGET-003 |

Publish an overview where spent exceeds budget. | The payload lets the view compute "nothing (or less than nothing) left to spend" so it renders the "Over budget" caption and accent — assert negative remaining survives the round trip rather than being clamped to zero. | n/a | App Group | n/a (local) | Unit | `src/features/budget/__tests__/budgetSnapshot.test.ts` | **CORNER** — negative amounts |

## Flagged / deferred (not scored)

Do **not** mark Pass/Fail for these in RESULTS until product or harness ships. Each entry names the concrete blocker so it can be closed, not just noted.

### A. Product / contract questions

| # | Topic | Why flagged | Affected rows |
|---|-------|-------------|---------------|
| 1 | **Money validation is duplicated seven times with three different rulesets** | There is no shared money-input component. `toCents` is re-implemented per screen and the rules disagree: the item form allows negative *and* zero; the spendings inline field rejects `<= 0`; goal form / registered / receipt reject `< 0` but allow zero; the recurring-payments field **strips the minus sign** so `-50` silently becomes `50`; the wish price field has no guard at all and can produce `NaN` cents. Product must pick one rule before these can be scored as pass/fail rather than "observed". | `BUDGET-CORNER-011`, `PLAN-059`, `PLAN-060`, `SPEND-040`, `SAVE-054`, `SAVE-064`, `WISH-027` |
| 2 | **Negative amounts reach the server before being rejected** | `budgetItemFormUtils.toCents` has no sign guard, so a typed `-50` is sent as `-5000` and rejected by Zod with a 400. Whether the user sees a usable error, or a generic failure, is unverified. | `BUDGET-PLAN-059` |
| 3 | **No decimal-place or length cap on any money input** | No field sets `maxLength`, and `keyboardType="decimal-pad"` still permits multiple `.` on iOS. `1.005` rounds to 101 cents silently; `1.2.3` truncates to `1.2`. Product to decide whether to reject or keep rounding. | `BUDGET-PLAN-062`, `PLAN-063`, `CORNER-012` |
| 4 | **Zero-amount policy is inconsistent across resources** | Zero is rejected for budget expenses and pension contributions but accepted for planned items, savings income/spending, goal targets and monthly goals. Believed intentional, never written down. | `BUDGET-CORNER-015` |
| 5 | **Budget chat assistant is hardcoded to Anthropic** | The `@assistant` path calls `resolveProviderApiKey(env, userId, 'anthropic')` with no provider selection, contradicting the multi-provider BYOK switch hub elsewhere in the app. A user whose only connected provider is OpenAI or Gemini gets silence. | `BUDGET-BCHAT-039` |
| 6 | ~~**Assistant entitlement denial is silent**~~ **RESOLVED 2026-07-22** — the assistant now inserts a visible notice ("AI access isn't available…") instead of silently dropping the reply (`insertAssistantNotice`). | `BUDGET-BCHAT-040`, `BUDGET-BCHAT-059` |
| 7 | **Worker-level Anthropic key gates the BYOK path** | The service checks `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` before resolving the user's own key, so a valid BYOK key still yields no reply when the worker-level key is absent. Looks unintended for a BYOK flow. | `BUDGET-BCHAT-039` |
| 8 | **Budget chat is not behind the Budget API gate** | `/budget-chat-rooms` is mounted outside `requireBudgetApi()`, so chat stays reachable on a Worker where `/budget`, `/savings` and `/wishes` all 404. May be intentional; undocumented either way. | `BUDGET-BCHAT-045` |
| 9 | **Year-setup discards an all-invalid save with no feedback** | Entering only invalid amounts filters everything out and the screen pops to root silently. | `BUDGET-SETT-026` |
| 10 | **Household photo presign route appears dead** | The client PUTs directly to `/households/:id/photo`; `POST /households/:id/photo/upload-url` has no caller. Either dead code or a half-migrated two-step upload. | `BUDGET-CORNER-020` |
| 11 | **Savings entry currency selector does not convert** | A currency can be chosen per entry but amounts stay raw integer cents with no FX applied, so mixed-currency households produce a meaningless total. | `BUDGET-SAVE-048` |
| 12 | **Registration is not reachable** | No sign-up entry point in this build; accounts are provisioned externally. Not a defect, but it means no fresh-account row can be scored. | `BUDGET-AUTH-012` |
| 13 | ~~**"Excluded from projections" has no projection engine to exclude from**~~ — **CLOSED 2026-07-25.** One-off income was barred from recurring income *templates* (`BUDGET-SAVE-081`/`083`) with no forecast to exclude it from. The **Savings → Projection** tab is now that forecast, and it honours the constraint recorded here: every forward figure (`paceMonthly`, `bestMonth`, `potentialMonthly`, the pace an untargeted month projects at) rides on **repeatable** net — the month's net minus its one-off income — while `actualNet`/`actualToDate`/the chart keep the windfall, because it really happened. `oneOffIncome` is returned per month and `excludesOneOffIncome` flags the year, so the UI explains the gap instead of showing a $22.5k month next to a $2.5k benchmark. | `BUDGET-SAVE-081`, `SAVE-083`, `SAVE-084`, `SAVE-104`…`107` |
| 14 | **`other` income is classified as regular, not one-off** | Semantically `other` is closer to one-off, but it predates the split and carries production rows, so reclassifying it would silently rewrite households' historical regular-vs-one-off analytics. Left as regular deliberately; product should confirm. | `BUDGET-SAVE-085` |

### B. Harness / testID blockers

| # | Topic | Why flagged | Affected rows |
|---|-------|-------------|---------------|
| 13 | **Three screens have zero testIDs** | `BudgetTimelineScreen`, `BudgetYearSetupScreen` and `BudgetChatRoomSettingsScreen` expose no testIDs on any control, so Maestro must select by visible text throughout. | `BUDGET-SETT-024`, `SETT-025`, `BCHAT-043` |
| 14 | ~~**Duplicate testIDs make selection ambiguous**~~ **PARTIALLY RESOLVED 2026-07-31** — of the three collisions this item originally named, two are fixed: `savings-goals-add` is now `savings-goals-add-empty`/`-list`, and `month-apply-status-${month}` is now `-changed`/`-applied`. The third, `budget-bills-view`, never existed under that name — the real collision is `utilities-screen`, reused across loading/error/empty/content on the shared `UtilitiesScreen`, but see item 31: that screen has no entry point in Budget's own tab config, so the live collision is unreachable from this brand rather than fixed. | `BUDGET-CORNER-019`, `BILL-031`, `SAVE-067` |
| 15 | **Derived, unstable testIDs** | Quick-add chips are `${prefix}-${suggestion.title}` and institution options are `-option-${inst.name}` — both change when content changes. | `BUDGET-DASH-039`, `SAVE-070` |
| 16 | **No `mention-assistant` selector** | `@assistant` is a first-class chat feature with its own flow file, but its mention row testID is derived from `user_id` like any member, so there is no stable selector for it. | `BUDGET-BCHAT-035` |
| 17 | **Untestable controls on otherwise-covered screens** | The add-household FAB, the swipe "Edit" action, "Delete/Leave Household", the create-room sheet's four controls, and the add-wish modal's title/description/cost inputs all lack testIDs. | `BUDGET-HH-017`, `HH-018`, `HH-022`, `BCHAT-029`, `WISH-024` |
| 18 | **Swipe delete confirmations lack testIDs** | Delete flows for planned items, spent entries and bills reach a confirm dialog that Maestro cannot address, leaving several rows at `gap`. | `BUDGET-PLAN-009`, `SPEND-048`, `BILL-009` |

### C. Environment / scope

| # | Topic | Why flagged | Target |
|---|-------|-------------|--------|
| 19 | **Dual-app Soft Transfer round trip** | Removed for standalone Budget — House↔Budget dual-sim round-trip is out of scope; transfer crypto is covered by `soft-transfer.test.ts` and the UI by the guarded `budget-soft-transfer-export/import` flows. | n/a (removed `BUDGET-ST-016`) |
| 20 | **Apple / Google OAuth** | Interactive 2FA blocks full automation. | Manual or seeded session · `BUDGET-AUTH-007`, `AUTH-009` |
| 21 | **Savings kill-switch** | The KV flag is rarely off in staging, so the 404 paths are hard to exercise live. | CORNER rows only · `BUDGET-SAVE-032`, `CORNER-001/002` |
| 22 | **Chat WebSocket assertions** | WS-delivered messages (including the assistant reply) are hard to assert in Maestro; the POST returns before the reply exists. | Unit + Live spot-check · `BUDGET-BCHAT-037` |
| 23 | **Heavy PDF fixtures** | BC assessment and property-tax upload extraction need large document fixtures. | API tests in `backend/src/routes/__tests__/utilities.test.ts` |
| 24 | **Receipt camera on CI** | The camera picker is stubbed on the simulator. | Gallery subflow only · `BUDGET-SPEND-033` |
| 25 | **Budget API disabled on the House Worker** | `BUDGET_API_ENABLED=false` makes every `/budget` route 404. | Budget-brand Worker only |
| 26 | **Pension `accounts` sub-view is not surfaced** | The code exists in `PensionView` but no tab reaches it — treat as `deferred`, do not write rows against it. | `BUDGET-PEN-034` |
| 27 | **`BudgetHomeScreen` is a dead surface for Budget** | It serves House's minimal mode; the Budget brand always renders the full dashboard. **It was also the only writer of `widget_budget_summary`, which is why the widget never rendered** — see item 29. | `BUDGET-HH-029`, `BUDGET-NAV-004`, `BUDGET-WIDGET-001` |
| 28 | **Utilities FE/BE gate mismatch (open defect)** | The client lets full-budget Budget into every `Utility*` screen while `gateHomeApiPaths` 404s `/households/:householdId/utilities`. Product must decide whether Budget owns utilities or not; the tests assert the current incoherent pair so the decision is forced rather than forgotten. | `BUDGET-CORNER-021` |
| 29 | **Budget watch + widget snapshot pipeline was dead (fixed 2026-07-20)** | `watch_budget_today` had no writer at all, and `widget_budget_summary`'s only writer (`BudgetHomeScreen`) is never mounted for Budget — so both native surfaces rendered their empty state permanently. Both are now published from the live dashboard and `clear()` wipes the `watch_*` keys. **Sibling status, verified:** Kaizen already had a working publisher (`kaizenWidgetSnapshot.ts`); Language and Health publish their *widget* keys but **not** their watch keys — `watch_language_today` and `watch_health_today` still have zero writers, so those two watch views remain dead. Out of scope for this Budget pass; tracked in [platform.md](./platform.md). | `BUDGET-WIDGET-001`, `BUDGET-WATCH-001`, `BUDGET-WATCH-007` |
| 30 | **On-device widget/watch rows stay `Live`** | Widget installation, timeline refresh, tap deep-link and paired-watch rendering cannot be driven by Maestro — it has no home-screen or watchOS surface. These four rows are scripted manual sim/device checks in `documents/engineering/testing/manual/budget-widget-watch.md`; everything upstream of them is unit-covered so the manual step only proves the last hop. | `BUDGET-WIDGET-010/011`, `BUDGET-WATCH-010` |
| 31 | **The entire BILL section's FE/Maestro automation was fabricated, and the screen has no entry point in Budget at all (found 2026-07-31)** | Every `budget-bills*.yaml` Maestro flow and `BudgetBillsView.test.tsx` unit-test citation in the BILL section pointed at files that never existed in this repo, and testIDs like `budget-bills-view` / `budget-bills-provider-*` / `budget-bills-action-*` do not exist anywhere in `src/` or `e2e/`. This goes beyond item 28's FE/BE gate mismatch: `brands/symply-budget/brand.cjs`'s `tabs` pool has **no** `utilities`/`bills` entry at all (not even hidden in the "More" overflow), and `app/(tabs)/bills.tsx` explicitly redirects `/bills` to Home with the comment "Bills is a House-domain feature… so it was removed from Budget — no brand pins a Bills tab." The real shared screens (`UtilitiesScreen`, `UtilityBillsScreen`, `PropertyTaxScreen`, …) and their real testIDs (`utilities-screen`, `utilities-provider-${key}`, `utilities-action-*`) still exist and are still reachable in **House**'s Maestro suite, but nothing schedules them for Budget. Product must decide: retire the whole BILL section as `deferred` (matching the tab removal), or restore a real entry point and author real Budget-tagged automation against the real testIDs. All 42 BILL rows were corrected in place 2026-07-31 (FE/Maestro citations → `gap`, BE/API citations untouched — those routes are real and brand-independent). | `BUDGET-BILL-001`…`042`, `CORNER-019`, `CORNER-021` |
| 32 | **Duplicate row IDs `BUDGET-SPEND-090`…`096` (found and fixed 2026-07-31)** | Two unrelated features — sales-tax attribution/multi-file receipts, and the spent-tab select-mode/batch-copy/batch-delete work — were both numbered `090`–`096`, in violation of the contract's "ID stable once published" rule (§2). The second block (select-mode) is renumbered to `097`–`103`; the one cross-reference (`BUDGET-PLAN-080` → `SPEND-091`) is updated to `SPEND-098`. | 2026-07-25 | **Savings → Projection tab** — added `SAVE-097`…`107`. The year on one screen: actuals for elapsed months, an editable goal for every month still ahead (one month or the whole rest of the year in one gesture), and the gap between the household's current pace and the pace they've already proven. New table `savings_monthly_targets` (migration 0117), `GET /savings/projection` + `PUT /savings/projection/targets`. **Closes flagged item 13** — this is the first deterministic forecast in the app, and it rides on repeatable net (one-off income excluded from the pace/benchmark, kept in the actuals) exactly as that flag required. 592 → 603 rows. |

Quick-add is unchanged on the Planning and Spending tabs (`PLAN-004`, `SPEND-003`). |

| 2026-07-25 | **Mortgage rate history & impact** — new `MORT` section, `MORT-060`…`072`. A variable/HELOC statement reports its rate as dated sub-periods, so the rate changes *mid-statement*; that breakdown lived only in the `raw_extraction_json` text blob (unqueryable). Promoted to `mortgage_rate_periods` (migration 0118, backfilled from the blobs via SQLite JSON1 + `json_valid` guard, idempotent) with `GET …/rate-periods`. History now merges statement-detected changes into the timeline (`rate_observed`, deduped against user-logged `rate_change` events) and adds the impact card built from the ACTUAL statement splits. Rows cover the persistence corner cases that bite: the shrinking-breakdown re-commit (delete-then-insert, else a phantom rate change), explicit orphan deletion (D1 may run without FK enforcement), and the scale-invariance invariant — conclusions come from interest SHARE, never a dollar delta, because statement periods are uneven. 603 → 616 rows. |

| 2026-07-29 | **Widget UI pass — savings trend, AI insight, `systemLarge`, real app-icon logo** — added `WIDGET-017`…`020`. `BudgetSummary`/`BudgetWidgetSnapshot` gain `savings_cents`/`savings_prev_cents` (from `SavingsOverview.netSavings` + the prior month's `SavingsTrendPoint.net`, looked up client-side — no new fetch) and `insight_tone`/`insight_message`/`insight_emoji` (reusing the dashboard's already-loaded `BudgetEncouragement` — no new AI call). `systemLarge` gets its own 2×2-figure dashboard layout (`SymplyBudgetWidgetContent.large`) instead of silently falling back to `medium`. The header now renders the real Budget app icon (new `BudgetAppIcon.imageset`, sourced from the existing `AppIcon-budget.appiconset`) via a new optional `SymplyHeader.logoImageName`, defaulting to `nil` so every other brand's widget is unaffected. New shared primitives `SymplyProgressBar` and `SymplyInsightBanner` in `WidgetTheme.swift`. 616 → 620 rows. |
| 2026-07-29 | **Renewal reminders for Monthly Payments** — added `SAVE-108`…`111`. A Monthly-Payments item (condo/car insurance, warranties, memberships, licenses) can now track a renewal — provider, reference number, cycle, next date, lead-days, optional attached documents — via new tables `budget_renewals`/`budget_renewal_documents` (migration 0145). Reuses two existing pieces of infra rather than building new ones: the shared "keep nagging until done" recurring-reminders engine (new `budget_renewal_reminder` registry type, `services/budget/renewal-reminder.ts` — no new cron trigger) already powering the mortgage statement nudge, and a household-scoped reserve→PUT-bytes→proxied-GET→delete attachment contract mirroring `health-assets.ts`'s file routes (Budget's own `receipt_key` turned out unused — extract-then-discard — so this is the first real attachment-persistence path in Budget). The Monthly Payments list gains a `renewal_summary` per item (one extra join, no N+1) and a "Renews in N days" row pill; the edit-payment modal gains a `RenewalReminderSection` (toggle → category/provider/cycle/date/lead-days → `ScanImportSources` attach → "Mark as renewed"). 620 → 624 rows. |
| 2026-07-29 | **Loan tracking for Monthly Payments** — added `SAVE-112`…`114`. A Monthly-Payments item (car loan, buy-now-pay-later plan, personal loan) can now track a loan — principal, 0%/fixed rate, term in months, start date — via new table `budget_loans` (migration 0146), a 1:1 sibling to `savings_recurring_payments` shaped like `budget_renewals`. All math (payments remaining, interest paid to date, total interest over the life of the loan) is computed on read, anchored to the payment's own `amount_cents` — never a second stored copy — by reusing the MORTGAGE amortization engine's closed-form functions (`services/mortgage/amortization.ts`) at MONTHLY compounding via a thin wrapper (`services/budget/loan-amortization.ts`); its `i === 0` branches already give exact 0%-APR math for a BNPL plan with no special-casing. `paymentsRemaining` is a plain `term_months − elapsed` subtraction anchored to the user-entered term rather than a solved root, since the household knows the term from the loan agreement. `loan_kind` reserves `'revolving'` (credit lines/cards — no fixed term, needs its own payoff-projection math) for a deliberately deferred follow-up; only `'installment'` ships here. The Monthly Payments list gains a `loan_summary` per item (one extra join, no N+1) and an "N payments left" row pill; the edit-payment modal gains a `LoanInfoSection` alongside `RenewalReminderSection` (toggle → rate type/principal/term/start date → summary card with a payoff progress bar). 624 → 627 rows. |

| 2026-07-31 | **`BUDGET-PLAN-009` actually enforced, plus a fleet-wide reporting fix.** The flow already called `e2e-matrix-before.yaml`/`e2e-matrix-after.yaml` but wired zero `E2E_VERIFY_*` env (and, separately, tagged the wrong row — `BUDGET-PLAN-010` — both fixed). Added `e2e-clear-log` before the delete drain loop and an explicit `e2e-verify-network` + `e2e-verify-badge` PASS assertion after it — Budget's own subflows already fire `e2e-verify-network` on ~25 other flows via `e2e-matrix-after.yaml`, but **none of them assert the badge afterward**, so a real verify FAILURE (confirmed live: `budget-spend-mutations.yaml` checks a non-existent route, `/budget/spent`, instead of the real `/budget/expenses`) is silently swallowed today — this row is the first fix, not a fleet-wide one. Also fixed `scripts/e2e/generate-report.mjs`, which silently dropped every `E2E-DB`/`E2E-R2`/`E2E-WS` log line from generated reports (only `E2E-NET`/`E2E-VERIFY`/`E2E-TAG` were captured), and added a new `e2e-verify-persist` capability (`src/api/e2eTestObservability.ts`, `e2e/maestro/subflows/e2e-verify-persist.yaml`) for brands whose mutations are local-first rather than network. |
| 2026-07-31 | **Full citation-accuracy pass (code-level/static verification only — no simulator this session).** Swept every section's Automation/UI-elements/BE-persistence citations against the actual repo: grepped every cited file path for existence (238 distinct paths), every cited testID-shaped string against `src/` + `e2e/` (335 candidates), and cross-checked HTTP method+path claims against `backend/src/routes/*.ts` for the newest features (sub-budgets, mortgage rate-periods, savings projection, renewals, loans, recurring-edit-scope propagate). **Most severe finding:** the entire `BILL` section (42 rows) cited automation that never existed — `e2e/maestro/budget/budget-bills.yaml` / `budget-bills-states.yaml` and `src/screens/budget/__tests__/BudgetBillsView.test.tsx` are not real files, and testIDs `budget-bills-view`/`budget-bills-provider-*`/`budget-bills-action-*` exist nowhere in the repo. Worse, the underlying screen has **no entry point in Budget at all**: `brands/symply-budget/brand.cjs`'s tab pool has no `utilities`/`bills` entry, and `app/(tabs)/bills.tsx` hard-redirects `/bills` to Home with a comment stating Bills was removed as a House-domain feature. All 42 rows corrected in place — FE/Maestro citations → `gap` with an explanatory note, real component names substituted (`UtilitiesScreen`/`UtilityBillsScreen`/`PropertyTaxScreen`), corrected testIDs where the real shared screen has them (`utilities-screen`, `utilities-provider-${key}`, `utilities-action-*`); the 23 BE/API rows were untouched since `backend/src/routes/utilities.ts` and its tests are real and brand-independent. New Flagged item 31 records this; see also the pre-existing item 28 it extends. **Second finding:** `BUDGET-SPEND-090`…`096` were fully duplicated — two unrelated features (sales-tax attribution/multi-file receipts, and spent-tab select-mode/batch-copy) both shipped under the same seven IDs, violating the "ID stable once published" contract. The second block is renumbered `097`…`103`; the one cross-reference (`PLAN-080`) is repointed. New Flagged item 32 records it. Also corrected: three stale testIDs (`SAVE-062`'s `savings-recurring-payments-add` → the real `savings-recurring-add`; `ST-020`'s fixed `household-picker-option-${id}` → the real `${testID}-option-${household.id}` pattern verified in `HouseholdPicker.tsx`) and two collisions Flagged item 14 / `CORNER-019` / `SAVE-067` had marked live but that later commits already fixed (`savings-goals-add` → `-empty`/`-list`; `month-apply-status-${month}` → `-changed`/`-applied` — only the `utilities-screen` collision, itself unreachable from Budget, is still live). **New coverage:** added `SAVE-115`/`116` for the recurring-payment edit-scope propagation feature (`POST …/recurring-payments/:id/propagate` — a 4-way "This month / This & future / Whole year / Keep past" prompt with real, already-passing FE (`SavingsRecurringPaymentsScreen.test.tsx`) and BE (`savings.test.ts`) tests) — the feature and its tests predate this pass but had zero matrix rows. Checked mortgage/sub-budgets/loan/renewal/projection/household-decoupling coverage named in the brief and found them already represented from prior passes. **Row-count audit:** the file's own "618 scored" tally (and the "589" in the Status field) had drifted silently across at least seven revisions that added rows without updating the counter — actual count is 745 (both corrected below). Did not touch any file outside this matrix. |

| 2026-08-04 | **Monthly Payments (Manage) list — removed inline loan progress card, loan rows now tap straight into the edit form.** Reworded `SAVE-114` (was: "shows a live loan pill", already-stale copy — the actual UI was a bigger progress card, not a pill) to guard the removal instead: the list row for a loan-tracked item no longer renders any progress card (`savings-recurring-loan-card`/`savings-recurring-loan-progress-bar` are gone), and tapping the row — like every other row — opens the full edit form directly rather than a read-only `LoanDetailBottomSheet` first. That sheet (and its dedicated test file) is deleted: every field it showed read-only (payments left, payoff date, interest paid, rate, lender, notes, portal link) is already on the edit form via `LoanInfoSection`, so it had become a pure middleman. `SAVE-118`'s focusItemId effect updated to match (always opens the edit form, never the now-deleted sheet). No row count change (114/118 edited in place, no rows added or removed). |
| 2026-08-04 | **Projection tab no longer floors a negative pace at $0.** `getProjection`'s `paceContribution` (fed to `projectedYearEnd`/`paceYearEnd` for untargeted future months) previously clamped a negative `paceMonthly` to 0 — "one bad month shouldn't project a bottomless year" — but that hid the true trajectory for a household on a persistent overspend, since income − monthly payments − spendings is legitimately negative. `paceContribution` now equals `paceMonthly` unclamped; `potentialMonthly` (the "Proven best"/stretch figure) changed from `max(0, paceMonthly, bestMonth.net)` to `bestMonth ? max(paceMonthly, bestMonth.net) : paceMonthly` — still never below the recent pace, but no longer floored at $0 when even the best month was a loss. Reworded `SAVE-103`/`105` in place (no row count change) and updated the two backend tests in `savings-projection.test.ts` that asserted the old $0 floor. |
| 2026-08-04 | **Home/Planning/Spending month nav now survives an app relaunch** — added `BUDGET-CORNER-027`. `budgetStore`'s `partialize` previously kept only `lastInsightsByHid`; `selectedYear`/`selectedMonth` are now included too, so the three tabs that share this one store (Home, Planning, Spending) reopen on the last-viewed month after a cold start instead of resetting to today. `activeView` and the insights dirty-flags stay deliberately session-only. Savings/Pension are unaffected — they carry their own independent, still-transient month state. |
| 2026-08-04 | **Year History / Projection / Compare Years were undercounting monthly payments — fixed cross-surface.** Added `BUDGET-SAVE-121`. `getYearHistory`'s `monthlyPayments` bucket only summed Budget expenses aliased into a "Monthly payments"/"Rent & Mortgage" category (the historical-import path); a household using the dedicated Recurring Payments feature without also duplicating those as Budget expenses got ZERO deduction for them here, so Year History, Compare Years, and everything Projection derives from `getYearHistory` (the month-by-month chart, "Saved so far", goal-attainment %, `projectedYearEnd`/`paceYearEnd`/`potentialYearEnd`) read inflated and disagreed with the Home dashboard's Cashflow-trend chart and Savings Overview for months that should have shown a real, negative deficit. Fixed by folding the household's active recurring payments (scoped via `isRecurringPaymentActiveInMonth`, the same source `getOverview`/`getTrend` use) into `monthlyPayments_m` alongside the category-aliased historical rows. This lands the same day as (and compounds correctly with) the `paceContribution`/`potentialMonthly` $0-floor fix above — untargeted future months now project off a TRUE pace instead of one previously computed from an undercounted `net`. |
| 2026-08-04 | **Monthly Payments "+" now opens a 2-option menu (Add manually / Import with AI); the standalone "Apply to months…" bulk-materialize flow is removed.** `SavingsRecurringPaymentsScreen`'s header `savings-recurring-add` no longer calls `openCreate` directly — it opens a small dismissible menu (`savings-recurring-add-manual` → the existing create form, `savings-recurring-add-import` → `SavingsImport` scoped `recurring`, same destination the old standalone `savings-recurring-ai-import` CTA and the below-list "Import monthly payments (AI)" button used). The "Apply to months…" `GradientButton` and its hint text are deleted along with their entry point into `MonthApplyModal`; since that modal had no other caller anywhere in the app, `MonthApplyModal.tsx` and `MonthApplyModal.test.tsx` are deleted outright as dead code rather than left orphaned. The `POST …/recurring-payments/apply` backend endpoint and its route test are untouched (still real, just callerless from the FE) — only the UI entry point is gone. `getRecurringApplyStatus` keeps its OTHER live caller (`openEdit`'s propagate-to-already-applied-months prompt, `SAVE-115`/`116`) unaffected. Reworded `SAVE-014`/`062` (new menu step) and `SAVE-015`/`066`/`067`/`068` (removal guards, `SAVE-066` narrowed to the endpoint's one remaining FE consumer) in place — no row count change. |
| 2026-08-16 | **Export became the fourth Sync & sharing destination, with a toggle per section.** Added `BUDGET-LF-016`; reworded `LF-002` (three rows → four) and `LF-006` (the CSV tap now routes through the new screen) in place. The `Export a spreadsheet (Excel)` `GradientButton` and the `Export raw data (CSV)` link sat under the Sync & sharing card's copy, which read as a footnote rather than a destination and left nowhere to ask what belonged in the file — they now live on `BudgetExportScreen` (`budget-settings-export-link` → `BudgetExport`), above a 14-row toggle list with `Enable all` / `Disable all`. The section keys are one shared vocabulary (`src/features/budget/local/export/exportSelection.ts`) mapping 1:1 to workbook tabs AND CSV files, so an excluded section is missing from both formats and from the Summary tab's tally; an absent or partial selection still means "include everything", which keeps every existing caller and any older stored preference on the full export. Choice persists under AsyncStorage `budget.export.sections`. The old testIDs `budget-settings-export-{xlsx,csv}` are unchanged but now resolve on the Export screen — `budget-local-first-settings.yaml` asserts they are NOT on Settings, and `budget-local-first-backup.yaml` taps through the row to reach them. 750 → 751 rows. |
| 2026-08-16 | **The recovery phrase got its own sheet, and stopped being copied without being asked.** Reworded `LF-012` and `LF-013` in place (no row count change). All three places that reveal the phrase — a manual backup, enabling automatic backup, and the `Recovery phrase` row — used `Alert.alert` with the twelve words run together in the body, and each one called `Clipboard.setStringAsync` first, so the phrase landed in a paste buffer every other app can read and the next copy overwrites, on a decision the user never made. They now open `BudgetRecoveryPhraseSheet` (`src/screens/budget/BudgetRecoveryPhraseSheet.tsx`): a `BottomSheet` with one numbered word per cell in a 2-column grid, a single accessible label so VoiceOver reads the phrase in order, and two explicit exits — `Copy` (which flips to `Copied` for 2.5s) and `Save as .txt`, the latter writing a numbered + one-line phrase file through the OS share sheet and deleting the plaintext copy from cache in `finally` (`src/features/budget/local/backup/recoveryPhraseFile.ts`). Presentation after the `files`/`share` destinations is delayed 400 ms, since iOS drops a modal presentation requested in the frame another one dismisses. Maestro's two alert-body regexes (`'Automatic backup on[\s\S]*sealed with this one phrase[\s\S]*'` and `'Saved on this device[\s\S]*Recovery phrase[\s\S]*'` + `tapOn: OK`) are replaced with the new testIDs, and `budget-local-first-auto-backup.yaml` now also taps `budget-backup-show-phrase` to prove the stored phrase is re-readable from the row. |
| 2026-09-03 | **SYNC & SHARING moved off Budget Settings onto Profile — one section, one home.** Reworded `LF-002` (Settings → Profile, four rows → five: Households joined the group), and `LF-003`/`LF-006`/`LF-007`/`LF-011`/`LF-012` where their steps opened the section from Settings. No row count change. Device Sync, Backup & Restore, Households, Invite & Household and Export answer "which copies of this budget exist and who holds them" — an account-and-device question — while Budget Settings keeps what shapes the budget (caps, categories, sub-budgets, transfers). The section is now `@features/budget/components/BudgetSyncSharingSection`, gated on `isFullBudget() && isBudgetLocalFirst()` so no other brand renders it. Profile is a sibling TAB of the one hosting the Budget stack, so every row navigates through `services/navigation` (`navigateToBudgetSync` / `…Backup` / `…Households` / `…Invite` / `…Export`), which re-enters the app by its own URL — an imperative push cannot deliver `screen=` to an already-mounted tab. `BudgetNavigator`'s `NavigationHandler` learned `screen=BudgetHouseholds` for the same reason. Every row testID is unchanged (`budget-settings-*`): they name the ROW, and 9 Maestro flows plus this matrix already speak them. New subflow `subflows/budget-open-profile.yaml` opens Profile and scrolls the section into view; `budget-local-first-settings.yaml` now also asserts Settings renders NONE of the rows. |

| 2026-09-10 | **Projection method picker + household default, and its Maestro coverage.** Added `SAVE-124`/`125` and `DASH-048`. The Projection tab now offers 4 forecast methods (`historical_average`/`trend`/`planned_budget`/`hybrid`, `PROJECTION_METHODS` in `src/api/savings.ts`) via `methodComparison` on the same `GET …/projection` response, each with an "i" `InfoButton` sheet explaining the formula in the household's own numbers where resolvable (`SavingsProjectionMonth.plannedBudget` breakdown: expected income / recurring payments / Planning-tab goal). A new household-wide `savings_projection_settings` table (migration 0172) backs "Set as household default" (`PUT …/projection/method`) — one row per household, not per member, so Home's new "Projected year-end" stat (beside "Savings this month" in the existing `budget-savings-headroom` card) and the iOS widget/Watch snapshot (`projected_year_end_cents`/`projected_year_end`) all read the SAME figure regardless of who set it. `budget-savings-projection-methods.yaml` is the first device coverage of the picker; it also closed a real gap — `DASH-010` had cited `budget-dashboard-controls.yaml` for `budget-savings-headroom` since this file's very first version, but no flow actually asserted it until now. |

**Row count (scored):** 795 — 792 (2026-08-22 recount) + 3: `SAVE-124`/`125` (Projection method picker + set-default) and `DASH-048` (Home's projected-year-end stat), added 2026-09-10 with `budget-savings-projection-methods.yaml` and an extension to `budget-dashboard-controls.yaml`; per-section: `SMOKE` 8 · `DASH` 42 · `PLAN` 122 · `SPEND` 106 · `SAVE` 124 · `PEN` 49 · `MORT` 13 · `BILL` 41 · `WISH` 31 · `BCHAT` 59 · `ST` 24 · `SETT` 32 · `HH` 28 · `AUTH` 15 · `CORNER` 27 · `NAV` 13 · `WIDGET` 20 · `WATCH` 10 · `LF` 16 · `MM` 15.
